Let's analyze previous program weaknesses:
- `fast_autocorr` uses A*A; correct for convolution.
- `lp_fw`: solves LP via cvxpy each iteration. Active set initial top 500, max 3500. It updates violations of conv_ys > M_curr + 1e-10. But it uses `g_dir` from LP that minimizes M subject to constraints for active set; line search with grid and bisection. It also initial K 500 for n~11700; each LP maybe heavy. Total time 1800 maybe mostly LP, maybe not enough iterations. Algorithm proposal:
1. Use continuous/small-scale global search:
   - For several lengths n (e.g., 2048, 4096, 8192, 16384), generate diverse initial sequences:
       * Previous height_sequence_1 interpolated.
       * Parametric families: exponential increase/decrease, sum of Gaussians, sine waves, piecewise linear, "arch" shapes, random smooth via Fourier basis with random coefficients, and optimized continuous small n.
   - Optimize each with a fast "peak shaving" multiplicative gradient + adaptive active top K, objective full convolution max.
2. Use a sophisticated "smooth line search" that uses softmax weights over active lags, computes gradient via FFT, performs mirror descent with normalized step, backtracking to ensure true objective decreases. This can be run thousands of iterations for n up to 32768.
3. Use "dual boosting" weights: maintain weights w over lags, update w to active peaks, compute gradient; use bregman projection (multiplicative). Possibly more effective than gradient of softmax at each step. We can alternate:
   - Compute c=conv(a,a).
   - Select active set / weights w proportional to exp((c - top)/temperature) for c near max.
   - Gradient g = 2*correl(w,a) + λ*1? For sum constraint, subtract mean gradient? Multiplicative update handles sum.
   - Try steps α in {1/t, ...}; compute new a = a * (1 - α*g)^+? Or a*exp(-α*g). Use true max to choose best. Since g can be all positive, exp reduces all, then normalize. This effectively shifts mass away from coordinates involved in active over-peaks.
   - Also try additive negative gradient with projection: a_new = a - α(g - mean(g))? But nonneg. Multiplicative better.

But the objective is homogeneous; multiplicative update with normalization is mirror descent on simplex for smooth objective. Need choose η small. Could use adaptive Polyak: η = best_gain / ||g||². We can evaluate a few step sizes.

To reduce active peaks globally, weights top K over all lags: Choose K maybe 5000? Computing gradient for K top lags naively O(K*n) too slow. But with full softmax over all lags, gradient via FFT O(n log n). Use w length 2n-1 full; compute gradient via FFT as above. - weights w=softmax(r/β_g) (normalized).

- Update g multiplicatively: g = normalize(g * exp(-η grad_g)).
- Repeat until r_max stabilizes. This is just same peak-shaver but with a fixed! Starting g=a. It should reduce max(a*g) significantly. Then direction g has lower cross peak. Gradient function:
```
def gradient_from_weights(w, a):
    n=len(a)
    L=len(w)
    N=1 << (L+n-2).bit_length()
    a_flip=a[::-1]
    F=np.fft.rfft(w,N)
    G=np.fft.rfft(a_flip,N)
    r=np.fft.irfft(F*G,N)[:L+n-1]
    return 2.0*r[n-1:n-1+n]
```
Need ensure w length L=2n-1. If w from c length L. Good.

Can optimize by computing FFT of a_flip each update? a changes; yes. w changes. Two rffts and irfft.

For inner subproblem (a fixed, g variable), gradient wrt g with fixed a: same function but roles? We need grad_g = 2∑_l w_l a_{l-i}. That's exactly `gradient_from_weights(w, a)` independent of g! The gradient of cross-conv a*g wrt g is the same with a (not g). So to update g, use grad_a_fixed. Good.

Two-level FW algorithm:
```
def fw_game(a0, time_limit, inner_iters=10, ...):
   a=normalize(a0); best...
   g=a.copy()
   for outer:
      c_a=conv(a); m_a=c_a.max(); update best
      for inner in range(inner_iters):
          r=conv(a,g)  # a fixed, g variable
          m_r=r.max()
          # stop if m_r close to? maybe after few.
          w=softmax(r/β_g)
          grad=gradient_from_weights(w,a)  # wrt g
          g=mul_update(g, grad, eta_g)
      alpha search: for α in [0.01..1] (maybe previous)
          t=(1-α)a+α g; t=normalize(max(t,0)); mt=conv(t).max(); if mt<current_m accept.
```

--- Detailed functions ---

```
def normalize_seq(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=1e-15:
        return np.ones_like(a)
    return a/s
```

But if we scale to sum 1, values ~1e-4, no clipping issue.

`resample_seq(a, n_new)`:
```
a=normalize_seq(a); old_n=len(a)
if old_n==n_new: return a
x_old=np.linspace(0,1,old_n)
x_new=np.linspace(0,1,n_new)
b=np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
# b may miss mass at boundaries? np.interp endpoint uses last. Fine.
return normalize_seq(b)
```
Could preserve oscillatory? okay.

`conv_coeffs(a)`:
```
n=len(a); L=2*n-1; N=1 << (L-1).bit_length()
A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
c=np.fft.irfft(A*A, N)[:L]
# Remove tiny negatives
np.maximum(c,0,out=c)
return c
```
Need `N=1 << (L-1).bit_length()`: if L=1, bit_length? For n=1, L=1, (L-1)=0 bit_length 0, 1<<0=1. okay. But for L=0 no. Use helper.

`score_full(a)`:
```
s=a.sum()
if s<=1e-15: return inf
c=conv_coeffs(a)
return 2*len(a)*c.max()/(s*s)
```
For normalized sum=1, use `score_norm`.

Cache? For optimization, we compute c often. Could return c. In peak_shaver, compute c each iter.

`softmax_weights(c, frac)`:
```
m=c.max()
if m<=0: return np.full_like(c,1/c.size)
beta=max(m*frac, 1e-300)
z=(c-m)/beta
z=np.clip(z, -50.0, 0.0)
w=np.exp(z)
s=w.sum()
if s<=0 or not finite: w=np.ones_like(c)/c.size
else: w/=s
return w
```
If frac small, weights mostly max lags. Good.

`gradient_weights(w,a)`:
Need weights length L. If w not length? Use resize? Ensure.
```
def gradient_weights(w,a):
    n=len(a)
    L=len(w)
    total=L+n-1
    N=next_pow2(total)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(w,N)
    af=np.fft.rfft(a[::-1],N)
    r=np.fft.irfft(fa*af,N)[:total]
    g=2.0*r[n-1:n-1+n]
    return np.maximum(g,0.0)
```
If w sum=1, gradient sum ~? okay.

`mul_update(a, grad, eta, floor)`:
```
la=np.log(np.maximum(a, floor))
la = la - eta * grad
# To maintain scale, subtract max before exp
la = la - la.max()
t=np.exp(la)
t=np.maximum(t, floor*1e-20?) # no, after exp tiny; maybe set exp underflow 0 okay then floor later.
s=t.sum()
if s<=0: t=np.ones_like(a)/len(a)
else: t/=s
# optional floor and renormalize:
t=np.maximum(t, floor)
t=t/t.sum()
return t
```
But adding `floor` after exp alters direction slightly; okay. floor maybe `1e-14 / n`? Could be too high for n=16k ->6e-19 negligible. Use `floor = 1e-30`? To avoid zero. Let's use `floor=1e-30` but log of a if zero -> -69? Actually log(1e-30)=-69, exp after updates can increase from floor but not from zero. If initial has exact zero, can grow to 1e-30, not significant. Maybe use `floor=1e-20/n` (~6e-25) small. Good.

However, if we hard floor after each update, the sum of floors n*floor ~1e-20, negligible. Good.

`peak_shaver(a0, t_limit, start_frac=0.35, end_frac=0.02, eta0=1.0, max_iter=None, seed=0, verbose=False)`:
Pseudo:
```
def peak_shaver(a0,t_limit,...):
    n=len(a0); a=normalize_seq(a0); best_a=a.copy(); best_m=conv_coeffs(a).max(); best_score=2*n*best_m
    t0=monotonic(); it=0; eta=eta0; frac=start_frac; no=0
    floor=1e-20/n
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    while time < t_limit:
       it+=1
       c=conv_coeffs(a); m=c.max()
       if m < best_m - 1e-15: best... no=0; if score < target: maybe continue
       else: no+=1
       if no>2000: # perturb
           a=normalize_seq(best_a + 0.02*rng.standard_normal(n)*best_a)
           continue
       frac = end_frac + (start_frac-end_frac)*np.exp(-it/1000)
       w=softmax_weights(c, frac)
       grad=gradient_weights(w,a)
       gmax=grad.max()
       if gmax<=0: break
       # scale eta? grad already with sum w=1. For n large, grad ~? We can normalize grad by gmax before eta, and eta dimensionless.
       gradn=grad/gmax
       # Try etas: eta, eta/2, eta*2 maybe
       best_trial_m=inf; best_a_t=None; best_eta=eta*0.5
       for e in [eta, eta*0.5, eta*2.0]:
           t=mul_update(a, gradn, e, floor)
           cm=conv_coeffs(t).max()
           if cm < best_trial_m:
               best_trial_m=cm; best_a_t=t; best_eta=e
       # Accept if best_trial_m < m (or m + 1e-12*m)
       if best_trial_m < m - 1e-15:
           a=best_a_t; eta=max(0.01, min(20.0, best_eta*1.05))
       else:
           # accept small if best_trial_m < m + 1e-10*m and it%...
           if best_trial_m < m + 1e-12*m:
               a=best_a_t
           eta*=0.8
           if eta<0.02:
              # random kick
              a=mul_update(a, gradn, 0.5, floor) maybe
       if verbose and it%100==0: print
    return best_a, 2*n*best_m
```
Issue: gradient normalized by max; step e dimensionless. If gradn max1, log update change up to e. e initial 1 may be big. Good.

def fw_search(a0, t_limit, inner=2, verbose):
   a=normalize(a0); g=a.copy()
   best...
   t0=...
   eta=1.0
   while time:
      c=conv(a); m_a=c.max(); update
      # inner loop for g
      for _ in range(inner):
         # conv(a,g)
         N=...; Fa fixed? compute Fa once per outer.
         Fg=fft(g,N); r=irfft(Fa*Fg)
         w=softmax(r, frac_g)
         grad=gradient_weights(w,a)  # wrt g, uses a
         g=mul_update(g, grad/gmax, eta_g, floor)
      # line search alpha
      alphas=[0.05,0.1,0.2,0.5,0.8,1.0]? but α=1 sets a=g may be risky.
      for α: t=(1-α)a+αg; t=normalize; mt=conv(t).max(); if mt<ma accept.
      if no improve: perturb g, reduce frac_g, or restart a=best
```

def coord_polish(a0,t_limit, pairs_per_round=20, fracs=(0.01,0.02,0.05,0.1)):
   a=normalize(a0); best...; t0
   stall=0
   while time:
      c=conv(a); m=c.max(); update
      w=softmax(c,0.1)
      grad=gradient_weights(w,a)
      # sources: top indices of a_i*grad_i (potential reduction)
      src_scores=a*grad
      srcs=np.argsort(src_scores)[-30:]
      # dests: indices with low grad; maybe also low a? Use pot=grad + 0.1*a? min
      dests=np.argsort(grad)[:30]
      improved=False
      for s in srcs:
         for d in dests:
            if s==d: continue
            if a[s] <= floor*10: continue
            for frac in fracs:
               amt=a[s]*frac
               t=a.copy(); t[s]-=amt; t[d]+=amt; t=np.maximum(t,0); ts=t.sum(); t/=ts
               mt=conv(t).max()
               if mt < m - 1e-15:
                   a=t; m=mt; best...; improved=True; break all fracs
            if improved: break
         if improved: break
      if not improved:
         stall+=1
         # random perturbation
         if stall%5==0: ...
      if stall>200: break
   return best
```

Let's draft code mentally.

```
import numpy as np
import time

def next_pow2(x):
    if x <= 1: return 1
    return 1 << (int(x)-1).bit_length()

def normalize_seq(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    s=a.sum()
    if s <= 1e-300:
       return np.ones_like(a)
    a=a/s
    # clip tiny
    return a

def resample_seq(a, n_new):
    a=normalize_seq(a)
    n_old=len(a)
    if n_old == n_new: return a
    if n_old == 1:
       return np.full(n_new, 1.0/n_new)
    xo=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n_old)
    xn=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n_new)
    b=np.interp(xn, xo, a)
    return normalize_seq(b)
```
Actually if n_old=1 and n_new maybe, could full uniform? But parent not.

```
def conv_coeffs(a):
    n=len(a)
    L=2*n-1
    N=next_pow2(L)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A, N)[:L]
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    return c
```
`next_pow2(L)` with L=32767 ->32768. For n=1, L=1 ->N=1. `rfft` n=1 okay.

```
def score_from_conv(a,c):
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=1e-300: return inf
    return float(2.0*len(a)*float(c.max())/(s*s))

def score_seq(a):
    c=conv_coeffs(a)
    return score_from_conv(a,c)
```

`softmax_weights(c, frac)`:
```
def softmax_weights(c, frac):
    m=float(c.max())
    if m <= 1e-300:
       return np.full_like(c, 1.0/c.size)
    beta = max(m*frac, 1e-300)
    z = (c - m)/beta
    np.clip(z, -50.0, 0.0, out=z)
    w = np.exp(z)
    s=w.sum()
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
       return np.full_like(c, 1.0/c.size)
    w /= s
    return w
```

`gradient_weights(w,a)` as above. Need if w length not 2n-1? It is. Use `N=next_pow2(len(w)+len(a)-1)`.

```
def gradient_weights(w, a):
    n=len(a)
    L=len(w)
    N=next_pow2(L+n-1)
    Fw=np.fft.rfft(w, N)
    Fa=np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], N)
    r=np.fft.irfft(Fw*Fa, N)[:L+n-1]
    g=2.0*r[n-1:n-1+n]
    np.maximum(g,0.0,out=g)
    return g
```
Make sure slice length n: r length L+n-1, indices n-1 to 2n-3 inclusive length n. If n=1: L=1,N=1, r length1, n-1=0 slice [0:1] good.

`mul_update(a, grad, eta, floor)`: grad assumed normalized? We can normalize inside to be safe. But if we normalize inside, eta dimensionless. Let's do:
```
def mul_update(a, grad, eta, floor):
    gmax=float(grad.max())
    if gmax <= 1e-300:
       return a.copy()
    d=grad/gmax
    la=np.log(np.maximum(a, floor))
    la = la - eta*d
    # rescale to avoid under/overflow
    maxla=float(la.max())
    if not np.isfinite(maxla):
       return a.copy()
    la = la - maxla
    t=np.exp(la)
    t=np.maximum(t, 0.0)
    s=t.sum()
    if s <= 1e-300:
       t=np.ones_like(a)
    else: t/=s
    t=np.maximum(t, floor)
    return t/t.sum()
```
If floor after normalize changes from t=1/n? okay. If eta large and some la very negative, exp underflow zeros, floor restores. Good.

Maybe use `floor=1e-25` global? For n=16k, floor 1e-25 negligible. In mul_update, if floor too small, log floor -57.5; fine. Use `FLOOR=1e-25`.

But further lower possible. Could break after a small polish. We'll not hard stop; just track.

  if verbose and (it%100==0): print

Candidate generation:
```
def make_param_candidates(n, seed_parent=None):
    outs=[]
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    i=np.arange(n,dtype=float)
    # uniform
    outs.append(normalize_seq(np.ones(n)))
    # linear inc/dec
    for a,b in [(0,1),(1,0)]:
       outs.append(normalize_seq(a + b*x)) # if b=1 sum positive; if a=0 okay
    # powers
    for p in [0.2,0.5,2,4]:
       outs.append(normalize_seq(x**p + 1e-12))
       outs.append(normalize_seq((1-x)**p +1e-12))
    # quasilinear with zero endpoints? x*(1-x) etc
    for p in [0.5,1,2]:
       outs.append(normalize_seq(x**p*(1-x)**p +1e-12))
    # exponentials
    for s in [-6,-3,-1,1,3,6]:
       vals=np.exp(s*x)
       outs.append(normalize_seq(vals))
    # gaussians
    for c in [0.2,0.3,0.5,0.7,0.8]:
       for sg in [0.1,0.2,0.35]:
          vals=np.exp(-0.5*((x-c)/sg)**2)
          outs.append(normalize_seq(vals))
    # double gaussians
    for c1,c2 in [(0.2,0.8),(0.3,0.7)]:
       for sg in [0.1,0.2]:
          vals=np.exp(-0.5*((x-c1)/sg)**2)+np.exp(-0.5*((x-c2)/sg)**2)
          outs.append(normalize_seq(vals))
    # cos
    for f in [1,2,3,5]:
       vals=np.maximum(1+0.8*np.cos(2*np.pi*f*x),1e-6)
       outs.append(normalize_seq(vals))
    # skewed polynomial
    # f = 1 + p*(x-0.5)+q*...
    for p in [-2,-1,0,1,2]:
       for q in [-1,0,1]:
          vals=1 + p*(x-0.5) + q*(x*(1-x)-1/6)
          vals=np.maximum(vals,1e-6)
          outs.append(normalize_seq(vals))
    # random smooth 20 with seed 1..20
    rng=np.random.default_rng(123)
    for r in range(20):
       vals = random_smooth(n, r)
       outs.append(vals)
    # parent
    if seed_parent is not None:
       outs.append(normalize_seq(seed_parent))
    return outs
```
Need no lambda. Function `random_smooth(n, seed)` top-level.

f=np.ones(n)
kmax=8
for k in range(1,kmax+1):
   amp=0.5/(k**0.7)
   ph1=rng.uniform(0,2π); ph2=...
   f += amp*np.cos(2πk*x+ph1)
   f += amp*np.sin(2πk*x+ph2)
# maybe add linear component
f += 0.8*rng.normal()* (x-0.5)
return normalize(np.maximum(f,1e-3))
```

```
def fw_like(a0, t_limit, inner=2, seed=7):
    n=len(a0); a=normalize_seq(a0); g=a.copy()
    best_a=a.copy(); best_m=conv_coeffs(a).max(); best_score=...
    t0=...
    eta_g=1.0; eta_a=0.5; frac_g=0.2
    no=0
    while time:
       c=conv_coeffs(a); m=c.max(); update
       if no>800: perturb g and a from best
       # precompute FFT of a for r and a_flip for gradient
       L=2*n-1; Nc=next_pow2(L)
       Fa=np.fft.rfft(a,Nc)
       # inner update g
       for _ in range(inner):
          Nsame=Nc
          Fg=np.fft.rfft(g,Nsame)
          r=np.fft.irfft(Fa*Fg,Nsame)[:L]
          np.maximum(r,0,out=r)
          w=softmax_weights(r, frac_g)
          grad=gradient_weights(w,a)  # uses Ngrad maybe larger
          g=mul_update(g, grad, eta_g, FLOOR)
       # line search alphas
       alphas=[0.1,0.2,0.5,0.8,1.0]
       best_trial_m=m; t_best=None; alpha_best=0
       for α in alphas:
          t=(1-α)*a+α*g
          t=normalize_seq(t)
          mt=float(conv_coeffs(t).max())
          if mt < best_trial_m - 1e-15:
              best_trial_m=mt; t_best=t; alpha_best=α
       if t_best is not None:
          a=t_best; no=0
       else:
          # maybe try small alpha 0.05
          t=0.95*a+0.05*g; ... if improve
          no+=1
       if verbose ...
    return best...
```
Cost: per outer inner times Fg+irfft + gradient (2 rffts+irfft) + 5 convs (rffts+irffts). Heavy. But if inner=1, maybe. We can reduce alphas to [0.2,0.5,1.0]. Use it for 200s only. Could be beneficial. If too slow, time check fails but returns best.

Alternative local improvement: Use "active lag deflation" by solving for a to reduce specific peaks: For each high lag l, move mass from contributing pairs. Could be faster and targeted. But gradient already.

Let's consider if peak_shaver will find continuous optimum. It is like mirror descent with gradient of weighted active peaks. Since problem nonconvex, but from good seed likely. The directed gradient uses weights over lags; as peaks flatten, weights spread. Good.

We can improve by using a "smooth objective gradient" with q instead of max+softmax. Our softmax weights is similar: w ∝ exp(c/β). Gradient of log-sum-exp with β=frac*m*? Actually logsumexp: w=exp(c/β)/sum. Our z=(c-m)/β = c/β - m/β; same. Good. The gradient of logsumexp is ∑ w c_l gradient. So `peak_shaver` is mirror descent on smoothed objective L_β(a). We line search on exact max. Good. β anneals. This is a solid method.

Could use Adam-like step: maintain exponential moving average of gradient? Maybe not.

Let's think of scaling of β. If β=m*frac, β shrinks as m shrinks. If m ~0.75/n ~5e-5, β for frac=0.05 ~2.5e-6. c differences near peak: active region width maybe? z differences of order β? Actually if c varies quadratically with slope, β small selects local peaks. Could be too small causing w on single lag. For fixed n=16k, we might want β a few coefficient widths. Starting frac 0.5, end 0.05 perhaps. If too small, local. We can adjust: use `frac_start=0.8, frac_end=0.08`. Let's choose moderate.        outs.append(normalize_seq(resample_seq(parent_seq, n)))

    outs.extend(base)

    return outs
```

`random_smooth`:
```
def random_smooth(n, seed):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    f=np.ones(n)
    for k in range(1,9):
       amp=0.35/(k**0.6)
       ph=rng.uniform(0, 2*np.pi)
       f = f + amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*k*x + ph)
       amp2=0.25/(k**0.6)
       ph2=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
       f = f + amp2*np.sin(2*np.pi*k*x + ph2)
    lin=rng.uniform(-1.0,1.0)
    f = f + lin*(x-0.5)
    f = np.maximum(f, 1e-6)
    return normalize_seq(f)
```
Maybe too wiggly can increase score. okay.

`score_candidates(cands)`:
```
def select_best_candidates(cands, limit):
    scored=[]
    for idx,a in enumerate(cands):
       if a is None: continue
       a=normalize_seq(a)
       sc=score_seq(a)
       if np.isfinite(sc): scored.append((float(sc), idx, a))
    scored.sort()
    out=[]
    for t in scored[:limit]: out.append(t[2])
    return out
```

```
def coord_polish(a0,t_limit,seed=99):
    n=len(a0); a=normalize_seq(a0); best=...; m=...
    t0=...; rng=default_rng(seed); stall=0
    fracs=(0.02,0.05,0.1)
    while time < t_limit:
       c=conv_coeffs(a); m=c.max(); update
       w=softmax_weights(c,0.08)
       grad=gradient_weights(w,a)
       # top sources
       src_score = a*grad
       # use top 6 positive
       srcs = np.argpartition(src_score, -6)[-6:] if n>6 else np.arange(n)
       # dest potential: low grad, but avoid moving to huge a maybe add 0.1*a
       pot = grad + 0.05*a
       dests = np.argpartition(pot, 6)[:6]
       improved=False
       for si in srcs:
          s=int(si)
          if a[s] < 1e-8: continue
          for di in dests:
             d=int(di)
             if d==s: continue
             for fr in fracs:
                amt=a[s]*fr
                if amt <=0: continue
                t=a.copy()
                t[s]=a[s]-amt
                t[d]=a[d]+amt
                np.maximum(t,0,out=t)
                ts=t.sum()
                if ts<=0: continue
                t/=ts
                mt=float(conv_coeffs(t).max())
                if mt < m - 1e-14:
                    a=t; m=mt; improved=True
                    break
             if improved: break
          if improved: break
       if improved:
          stall=0
       else:
          stall+=1
          if stall % 5 ==0:
             # random move
             if n>1:
                i=int(rng.integers(0,n)); j=int(rng.integers(0,n))
                if i!=j and a[i]>1e-8:
                   amt=a[i]*0.02
                   t=a.copy(); t[i]-=amt; t[j]+=amt; t=np.maximum(t,0); ts=t.sum(); t/=ts
                   mt=conv_coeffs(t).max()
                   if mt < m: a=t; m=mt; improved=True
          if stall > 300: break
    return best...
```

def make_main_candidates(n, parent, small_best):
   outs=[]
   if parent is not None: outs.append(resample_seq(parent,n))
   if small_best is not None: outs.append(resample_seq(small_best,n))
   x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   # linear inc/dec, powers, exponentials, gaussians (few)
   outs.append(normalize_seq(np.ones(n)))
   outs.append(normalize_seq(x+1e-12))
   outs.append(normalize_seq(1.0-x+1e-12))
   for p in [1.0,2.0]: ...
   for s in [-3,-1,1,3]: exp
   for c,sg in [(0.2,0.2),(0.8,0.2),(0.5,0.3)]: gaussian
   # double gaussians
   for r in [11,12,13]: outs.append(random_smooth(n,r))
   return outs
```

Let's now think through `gradient_weights` orientation one more time. For w length L and a length n, derivative c_l wrt a_i = 2 a_{l-i} if valid. For each i, sum over l w_l a_{l-i}. We compute `r = conv(w, a_flip)`; index m=n-1+i: r[m]=∑_{j} w_j a_flip[m-j]=∑_l w_l a_flip[n-1+i-l]=∑_l w_l a_{n-1 - (n-1+i-l)} =∑_l w_l a_{l-i}. Yes. If l-i outside 0..n-1, a_flip index outside -> 0 in padded. Good. Factor 2. So correct.

Convolution c: A*A coefficient l =∑ a_i a_{l-i}. For real, `irfft(A*A)` correct. Good.

`load_parent`:
```
def load_parent():
    raw = globals().get('height_sequence_1', None)
    if raw is None: return None
    try:
       arr=np.asarray(raw, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
       return None
    if arr.size ==0: return None
    arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr=np.maximum(arr,0.0)
    s=arr.sum()
    if s <=1e-300: return None
    arr=arr/s
    return arr
```

`eval_safe`:
```
def eval_safe(a):
    try:
       lst=[float(x) for x in a]
       v=evaluate_sequence(lst)
       if v is not None and np.isfinite(v): return float(v)
    except Exception:
       pass
    return float('inf')
```

def remaining_time(t0, budget, reserve=30.0):

def propose_candidate(budget_s=1800.0):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget = min(float(budget_s) - 90.0, 1710.0)
    except Exception:
        budget=1710.0
    if budget < 30:
       b=np.ones(1024)/1024; return [float(x) for x in b]
    print("Total budget: %.1f" % budget)
    parent = load_parent()
    actual_best = float('inf'); actual_best_seq=None
    if parent is not None:
       pa=eval_safe(parent)
       if np.isfinite(pa): actual_best=pa; actual_best_seq=parent
       print("parent local? actual", actual_best)
    # small
    small_n=1024 if budget>800 else 512
    small_t=min(150.0, budget*0.12)
    t_phase=time.monotonic()
    print("Small search n=", small_n)
    small_cands = make_small_candidates(small_n, parent)
    small_sel = select_best_candidates(small_cands, limit=6)
    best_small=None; best_small_score=float('inf')
    if small_sel:
       n_run=min(len(small_sel), max(2, int(small_t//15)+1))
       per = small_t / max(1,n_run)
       for idx,a in enumerate(small_sel[:n_run]):
          if time.monotonic() - t_phase > small_t: break
          tt=min(per, 40.0)
          if tt <2: tt=2
          b,sc=run_peak_shaver(a, tt, seed=100+idx, frac_start=0.5, frac_end=0.08, eta0=1.0, verbose=False)
          if sc < best_small_score:
             best_small_score=sc; best_small=b
    # main candidates
    main_n = 16384 if budget > 800 else (8192 if budget>300 else 4096)
    main_cands = make_main_candidates(main_n, parent, best_small)
    main_sel = select_best_candidates(main_cands, limit=3)
    if not main_sel:
       main_sel=[np.ones(main_n)/main_n]
    best_seq=main_sel[0].copy(); best_score=score_seq(best_seq)
    print("Initial main n=%d score=%.7f" % (main_n,best_score))
    t_main=time.monotonic()
    main_t = min(budget*0.45, 700.0)
    # run on best
    rem=main_t - (time.monotonic()-t_main)
    if rem>20:
       b,sc=run_peak_shaver(best_seq, rem, seed=7, frac_start=0.5, frac_end=0.05, eta0=0.8, verbose=True)
       if sc < best_score - 1e-12: best_score=sc; best_seq=b
    # second candidate
    rem_total = budget - (time.monotonic()-t0)
    if len(main_sel)>1 and rem_total>100:
       tt=min(rem_total*0.18, 200.0)
       b,sc=run_peak_shaver(main_sel[1], tt, seed=17, verbose=False)
       if sc < best_score: best_score=sc; best_seq=b
    # third
    rem_total = budget - (time.monotonic()-t0)
    if len(main_sel)>2 and rem_total>80:
       tt=min(rem_total*0.12, 120.0)
       b,sc=run_peak_shaver(main_sel[2], tt, seed=27, verbose=False)
       if sc < best_score: ...
    # maybe re-run best with less aggressive params if time
    rem_total = budget - (time.monotonic()-t0)
    if rem_total>200:
       tt=min(rem_total*0.25, 300.0)
       b,sc=run_peak_shaver(best_seq, tt, seed=37, frac_start=0.35, frac_end=0.03, eta0=0.6, verbose=True)
       update
    # FW
    rem_total = budget - (time.monotonic()-t0)
    if rem_total>250:
       tt=min(rem_total*0.30, 300.0)
       try:
          b,sc=fw_like(best_seq, tt, verbose=False)
          if sc < best_score: update
       except Exception: print
    # coord
    rem_total = budget - (time.monotonic()-t0)
    if rem_total>120:
       tt=min(rem_total*0.35, 300.0)
       b,sc=coord_polish(best_seq, tt, seed=123)
       update
    # extra length
    rem_total = budget - (time.monotonic()-t0)
    if rem_total>180:
       n_extra = 8192 if main_n>8192 else 4096
       a_ex=resample_seq(best_seq,n_extra)
       tt=min(rem_total*0.4, 150.0)
       b,sc=run_peak_shaver(a_ex, tt, seed=57, verbose=False)
       if sc < best_score: update (but different n; okay)
    # final actual compare
    fs=eval_safe(best_seq)
    if np.isfinite(fs): final_actual=fs
    else: final_actual=inf
    if actual_best_seq is not None and actual_best < final_actual:
         final_seq=actual_best_seq; final_actual=actual_best
    else: final_seq=best_seq
    # ensure finite
    final_seq=normalize_seq(final_seq)
    # If length too big? maybe not. If length > 20000, resample to 16384 to ensure eval speed? But if best from extra n=8192 okay. If main 16384 okay.
    if len(final_seq) > 20000:
         final_seq=resample_seq(final_seq,16384)
    ff=eval_safe(final_seq)
    if not np.isfinite(ff): final_seq=np.ones(4096)/4096
    print final
    return [float(x) for x in final_seq]

Base candidates list generation code carefully no lambdas:
```
def make_base_candidates(n):
   outs=[]
   x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
   # uniform
   outs.append(normalize_seq(np.ones(n)))
   # linear inc dec
   vals=x+1e-12; outs.append(normalize_seq(vals))
   vals=1.0-x+1e-12; outs.append(normalize_seq(vals))
   for p in (0.5,1.0,2.0,4.0):
       vals=np.power(x,p)+1e-12; outs.append(normalize_seq(vals))
       vals=np.power(1.0-x,p)+1e-12; outs.append(normalize_seq(vals))
   for p in (0.5,1.0,2.0):
       vals=np.power(x,p)*np.power(1.0-x,p)+1e-12; outs.append(normalize_seq(vals))
   for s in (-6.0,-3.0,-1.0,1.0,3.0,6.0):
       vals=np.exp(s*x); outs.append(normalize_seq(vals))
   # shifted exponentials with zero at one end? x^? already.
   for c in (0.15,0.3,0.5,0.7,0.85):
       for sg in (0.08,0.18,0.35):
          vals=np.exp(-0.5*((x-c)/sg)**2); outs.append(normalize_seq(vals))
   for c1,c2 in ((0.2,0.8),(0.3,0.7),(0.1,0.9)):
       for sg in (0.1,0.22):
          vals=np.exp(-0.5*((x-c1)/sg)**2)+np.exp(-0.5*((x-c2)/sg)**2)
          outs.append(normalize_seq(vals))
   for f in (1,2,3,5):
       vals=np.maximum(0.2 + np.cos(2.0*np.pi*f*x), 1e-8)
       outs.append(normalize_seq(vals))
       vals=np.maximum(0.5 + 0.5*np.sin(2.0*np.pi*f*x), 1e-8)
       outs.append(normalize_seq(vals))
   # polynomials zero-mean
   for p in (-2.0,-1.0,-0.5,0.5,1.0,2.0):
       for q in (-1.0,0.0,1.0):
          vals=1.0 + p*(x-0.5) + q*(x*(1.0-x)-1.0/6.0)
          vals=np.maximum(vals,1e-8)
          outs.append(normalize_seq(vals))
   return outs
```
Potential `np.power(x,0.5)` at x=0 okay. `x=0` and p=0? no p>0.
Number candidates: uniform 1 + linear2 + powers8 + product3 + exp6 + gaussians15 + double9 + cos8 + poly18 =70. Small fine. Main if use base all 70 scoring at 16k maybe 70* (32k FFT) okay maybe 70*0.02=1.4s, fine. But selecting main from all base maybe good. Detailed `run_peak_shaver` code:
```
def run_peak_shaver(a0, t_limit, seed=1, frac_start=0.5, frac_end=0.05, eta0=1.0, verbose=False):
    n=len(a0)
    a=normalize_seq(a0)
    best_a=a.copy()
    c=conv_coeffs(a)
    best_m=float(c.max())
    best_score=2.0*n*best_m
    t0=time.monotonic()
    end=t0+max(0.1,t_limit)
    it=0; no_imp=0; eta=eta0; rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    floor=FLOOR
    while time.monotonic() < end:
       it+=1
       if not np.isfinite(best_m): best_m=1e100; best_a=best_a.copy() if ...
       c=conv_coeffs(a)
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(c)):
          a=best_a.copy(); no_imp+=100; continue
       m=float(c.max())
       if m < best_m - 1e-15:
          best_m=m; best_a=a.copy(); no_imp=0
       else:
          no_imp+=1
       if no_imp > 800:
          a=normalize_seq(best_a + 0.03*rng.standard_normal(n)*(best_a+1e-9*best_a.max()))
          eta=max(eta0, 0.5)
          no_imp=0
          continue
       # frac schedule
       progress=min(1.0, float(it)/3000.0)
       frac = frac_start - (frac_start-frac_end)*progress
       # active adjust later
       w=softmax_weights(c, frac)
       # active count adjust
       if it % 25 ==0:
          wmax=float(w.max())
          if wmax>0:
             cnt=int(np.sum(w > 1e-7*wmax))
             if cnt < 30:
                frac=min(frac_start, frac*1.6)
             elif cnt > 0.8*(2*n-1):
                frac=max(frac_end, frac*0.8)
             if frac != ...: w=softmax_weights(c, frac)
       grad=gradient_weights(w,a)
       gmax=float(grad.max())
       if gmax <= 1e-300:
          a=best_a.copy(); no_imp=0; continue
       grad_n=grad/gmax
       best_trial_m=1e300; best_trial=None; best_eta=eta
       # choose etas maybe scaled by n? frac? no
       etas=(eta, 0.5*eta, 2.0*eta)
       for e in etas:
          t=mul_update(a, grad_n, e, floor)
          ct=conv_coeffs(t)
          mt=float(ct.max())
          if mt < best_trial_m:
             best_trial_m=mt; best_trial=t; best_eta=e
       if best_trial is None:
          a=best_a.copy(); eta=max(0.02, eta*0.7); continue
       if best_trial_m < m - 1e-15:
          a=best_trial
          eta=min(15.0, max(0.02, best_eta*1.05))
       else:
          if best_trial_m < m + 1e-12*m:
             a=best_trial
          eta=max(0.02, eta*0.85)
          if eta < 0.05 and no_imp > 50:
             a=normalize_seq(best_a + 0.02*rng.standard_normal(n)*(best_a+1e-9*best_a.max()))
             eta=eta0
             no_imp=0
       if verbose and (it % 200 == 1):
          print("  Peak n=%d it=%d score=%.7f" % (n,it,2.0*n*best_m))
    return best_a, 2.0*n*best_m
```
Potential issue: If initial a has sum 1, best_a copy. When perturbing, use `best_a.max()` maybe 0 if all zero? best_a normalized sum1 so max>0.
`np.all(np.isfinite(c))` maybe costly? c size 32k, okay. But every iter maybe extra. Could skip; FFT won't produce inf unless input inf. Input finite. Remove for speed. Use if `not np.isfinite(m)`.

If m is finite but c has tiny negative clipped, okay.

Active count recomputes w; okay.

def fw_like(a0, t_limit, seed=3, verbose=False):
    n=len(a0); a=normalize_seq(a0); g=a.copy()
    c=conv_coeffs(a); best_m=c.max(); best_a=a.copy(); best_score=...
    t0=...; end=...
    it=0; no=0; eta_g=1.0; frac_g=0.3; L=2*n-1; Nc=next_pow2(L); floor=FLOOR
    rng=...
    while time<end:
       it+=1
       c=conv_coeffs(a); m=c.max()
       if m<best_m-... update
       else no+=1
       if no>500:
          a=normalize(best_a + 0.05*rng...*(...)); g=a.copy(); frac_g=0.3; no=0; continue
       Fa=np.fft.rfft(a,Nc)
       # inner 2? Let's do 2 but check time? inner = 2 if n<10000 else 1
       inner = 2 if n <= 10000 else 1
       for _ in range(inner):
          Fg=np.fft.rfft(g,Nc)
          r=np.fft.irfft(Fa*Fg,Nc)[:L]
          np.maximum(r,0,out=r)
          frac = frac_g - (frac_g-0.06)*min(1.0,it/3000.0)
          w=softmax_weights(r, frac)
          grad=gradient_weights(w,a)
          g=mul_update(g, grad, eta_g, floor)
       # line search
       alphas=(0.2,0.5,0.8,1.0)
       best_mt=m; t_best=None; best_alpha=0
       for al in alphas:
          t=(1.0-al)*a + al*g
          t=normalize_seq(t)
          mt=float(conv_coeffs(t).max())
          if mt < best_mt - 1e-15:
             best_mt=mt; t_best=t; best_alpha=al
       if t_best is not None:
          a=t_best
          if best_alpha>=0.8: eta_g=min(8.0, eta_g*1.1)
          else: eta_g=max(0.05, eta_g*0.8)
          no=0
       else:
          no+=1
          eta_g=max(0.05, eta_g*0.9)
          # occasionally perturb g
          if no % 20 ==0:
             g=normalize_seq(g + 0.1*rng.standard_normal(n)*g)
       if verbose and it%100==1: print
    return best...

def softmax_weights(c, frac):
    if frac <=0: frac=0.5
    m=float(c.max())
    L=c.size
    if m <= 1e-300:
       return np.full(L, 1.0/L)
    beta=m*frac
    if beta <= 0: beta=1e-300
    z=(c-m)/beta
    np.clip(z, -50.0, 0.0, out=z)
    w=np.exp(z)
    s=float(w.sum())

       return np.full(L, 1.0/L)
    w /= s
    return w

def mul_update(a, grad, eta, floor):
    a=np.asarray(a)
    gmax=float(grad.max())
    if gmax <= 1e-300 or eta <=0:
       return a.copy()
    d=grad/gmax
    la=np.log(np.maximum(a, floor))
    la = la - eta*d
    maxla=float(la.max())
    if not np.isfinite(maxla):
       return a.copy()
    la=la-maxla
    t=np.exp(la)
    s=float(t.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
       return a.copy()
    t=t/s
    t=np.maximum(t, floor)
    s2=float(t.sum())
    if s2 <= 1e-300:
       return a.copy()
    return t/s2

```python
"""
Search for non-negative step-function heights minimizing

    2 * n * max(convolve(a, a)) / (sum(a))^2

using:
- fast FFT-based convolution with continuation on the active peaks
- mirrored search-guided subgradient updates
- a lightweight Frank-Wolfe-like direction solver
- short local coordinate polish
- multi-scale initialisation from the previous state of the art if available

The returned sequence is normalized to sum 1, which is equivalent under the
given evaluation formula (away from the enforced 0 / 1000 clipping range).
"""

import numpy as np
import time

FLOOR = 1e-25


def next_pow2(x):
    x = int(x)
    if x <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (x - 1).bit_length()


def normalize_seq(a):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(arr.size, dtype=np.float64) / max(arr.size, 1)
    return arr / s


def load_parent():
    raw = globals().get('height_sequence_1', None)
    if raw is None:
        return None
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(raw, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return None
    if arr.size == 0:
        return None
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return None
    return arr / s


def resample_seq(a, n_new):
    n_new = int(n_new)
    arr = normalize_seq(a)
    n_old = arr.size
    if n_new <= 1:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    if n_old == n_new:
        return arr
    if n_old <= 1:
        return np.full(n_new, 1.0 / n_new, dtype=np.float64)
    xo = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_old)
    xn = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_new)
    b = np.interp(xn, xo, arr)
    return normalize_seq(b)


def conv_coeffs(a):
    n = len(a)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    N = next_pow2(L)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)
    if c.size < L:
        c = np.resize(c, L)
    else:
        c = c[:L]
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    return c


def score_seq(a):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return float('inf')
    c = conv_coeffs(arr)
    m = float(c.max())
    return float(2.0 * len(arr) * m / (s * s))


def softmax_weights(c, frac):
    if frac <= 0.0:
        frac = 0.5
    L = c.size
    m = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(L, 1.0 / max(L, 1), dtype=np.float64)
    beta = m * frac
    if beta <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(beta):
        beta = 1e-300
    z = (c - m) / beta
    np.clip(z, -50.0, 0.0, out=z)
    w = np.exp(z)
    s = float(w.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(L, 1.0 / max(L, 1), dtype=np.float64)
    return w / s


def gradient_weights(w, a):
    n = len(a)
    L = len(w)
    N = next_pow2(L + n - 1)
    Fw = np.fft.rfft(w, N)
    Fa = np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], N)
    r = np.fft.irfft(Fw * Fa, N)
    total = L + n - 1
    if r.size < total:
        r = np.resize(r, total)
    else:
        r = r[:total]
    g = 2.0 * r[n - 1:n - 1 + n]
    np.maximum(g, 0.0, out=g)
    return g


def mul_update(a, grad, eta, floor):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    gmax = float(grad.max())
    if gmax <= 1e-300 or eta <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(eta):
        return a.copy()
    d = grad / gmax
    la = np.log(np.maximum(a, floor))
    la = la - eta * d
    mx = float(la.max())
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        return a.copy()
    la = la - mx
    t = np.exp(la)
    s = float(t.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return a.copy()
    t = t / s
    t = np.maximum(t, floor)
    s2 = float(t.sum())
    if s2 <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s2):
        return a.copy()
    return t / s2


def run_peak_shaver(a0, t_limit, seed=1, frac_start=0.5, frac_end=0.05,
                    eta0=1.0, verbose=False):
    a = normalize_seq(a0)
    n = len(a)
    c = conv_coeffs(a)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_m = float(c.max())
    if t_limit <= 0.0 or n <= 0:
        return best_a, float(2.0 * n * best_m)

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    end = t0 + float(t_limit)
    it = 0
    no_improve = 0
    eta = float(eta0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    floor = FLOOR

    while time.monotonic() < end:
        it += 1
        c = conv_coeffs(a)
        m = float(c.max())
        if not np.isfinite(m):
            a = best_a.copy()
            c = conv_coeffs(a)
            m = float(c.max())

        if m < best_m - 1e-15:
            best_m = m
            best_a = a.copy()
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        if no_improve > 900:
            nz = 0.03 * rng.standard_normal(n) * (best_a + 1e-6 * float(best_a.max()) + 1e-12)
            a = normalize_seq(best_a + nz)
            eta = float(eta0)
            no_improve = 0
            continue

        progress = min(1.0, float(it) / 1500.0)
        frac = frac_start - (frac_start - frac_end) * progress

        w = softmax_weights(c, frac)
        if it % 25 == 0:
            wmax = float(w.max())
            if wmax > 0.0 and np.isfinite(wmax):
                cnt = int(np.sum(w > 1e-7 * wmax))
                L = 2 * n - 1
                if cnt < 30:
                    frac = min(frac_start, frac * 1.6)
                elif cnt > 0.8 * L:
                    frac = max(frac_end, frac * 0.8)
                else:
                    frac = min(frac_start, max(frac_end, frac))
                if np.isfinite(frac) and frac > 0.0:
                    w = softmax_weights(c, frac)
                else:
                    frac = 0.25
                    w = softmax_weights(c, frac)

        grad = gradient_weights(w, a)
        gmax = float(grad.max())
        if not np.isfinite(gmax) or gmax <= 1e-300:
            a = best_a.copy()
            no_improve = 0
            continue

        grad_n = grad / gmax
        best_trial_m = float('inf')
        best_trial = None
        best_eta = eta

        for e in (eta, 0.5 * eta, 2.0 * eta):
            t = mul_update(a, grad_n, e, floor)
            cm = float(conv_coeffs(t).max())
            if np.isfinite(cm) and cm < best_trial_m:
                best_trial_m = cm
                best_trial = t
                best_eta = float(e)

        if best_trial is None:
            a = best_a.copy()
            eta = max(0.02, 0.7 * eta)
            continue

        if best_trial_m < m - 1e-15:
            a = best_trial
            if best_trial_m < best_m - 1e-15:
                best_m = best_trial_m
                best_a = a.copy()
            eta = min(15.0, max(0.02, 1.05 * best_eta))
        else:
            if best_trial_m < m + 1e-12 * max(1.0, m):
                a = best_trial
            eta = max(0.02, 0.85 * eta)
            if eta < 0.05 and no_improve > 50:
                nz = 0.02 * rng.standard_normal(n) * (best_a + 1e-6 * float(best_a.max()) + 1e-12)
                a = normalize_seq(best_a + nz)
                eta = float(eta0)
                no_improve = 0

        if verbose and (it % 200 == 1):
            print("  peak n=%d it=%d score=%.7f" % (n, it, 2.0 * best_m))

    return best_a, float(2.0 * n * best_m)


def fw_like(a0, t_limit, seed=3, verbose=False):
    a = normalize_seq(a0)
    n = len(a)
    c = conv_coeffs(a)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_m = float(c.max())
    if t_limit <= 0.0 or n <= 0:
        return best_a, float(2.0 * n * best_m)

    g = a.copy()
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    end = t0 + float(t_limit)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    Nc = next_pow2(L)
    it = 0
    no_improve = 0
    eta_g = 1.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    inner = 2 if n <= 8000 else 1

    while time.monotonic() < end:
        it += 1
        c = conv_coeffs(a)
        m = float(c.max())
        if not np.isfinite(m):
            a = best_a.copy()
            g = a.copy()
            continue

        if m < best_m - 1e-15:
            best_m = m
            best_a = a.copy()
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        if no_improve > 500:
            nz = 0.05 * rng.standard_normal(n) * (best_a + 1e-6 * float(best_a.max()) + 1e-12)
            a = normalize_seq(best_a + nz)
            g = a.copy()
            eta_g = 1.0
            no_improve = 0
            continue

        Fa = np.fft.rfft(a, Nc)

        for _ in range(inner):
            Fg = np.fft.rfft(g, Nc)
            r = np.fft.irfft(Fa * Fg, Nc)[:L]
            np.maximum(r, 0.0, out=r)
            progress = min(1.0, float(it) / 2500.0)
            frac = 0.30 - (0.30 - 0.06) * progress
            w = softmax_weights(r, frac)
            grad = gradient_weights(w, a)
            g = mul_update(g, grad, eta_g, FLOOR)

        best_trial_m = m
        best_trial = None
        best_alpha = 0.0
        for alpha in (0.1, 0.3, 0.6, 1.0):
            t = (1.0 - alpha) * a + alpha * g
            t = normalize_seq(t)
            mt = float(conv_coeffs(t).max())
            if np.isfinite(mt) and mt < best_trial_m - 1e-15:
                best_trial_m = mt
                best_trial = t
                best_alpha = float(alpha)

        if best_trial is not None:
            a = best_trial
            if best_trial_m < best_m - 1e-15:
                best_m = best_trial_m
                best_a = a.copy()
            no_improve = 0
            if best_alpha >= 0.8:
                eta_g = min(8.0, 1.1 * eta_g)
            else:
                eta_g = max(0.05, 0.85 * eta_g)
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            eta_g = max(0.05, 0.9 * eta_g)
            if no_improve % 20 == 0:
                g = normalize_seq(g + 0.1 * rng.standard_normal(n) * g)

        if verbose and (it % 100 == 1):
            print("  fw n=%d it=%d score=%.7f" % (n, it, 2.0 * best_m))

    return best_a, float(2.0 * n * best_m)


def coord_polish(a0, t_limit, seed=99, verbose=False):
    a = normalize_seq(a0)
    n = len(a)
    c = conv_coeffs(a)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_m = float(c.max())
    if t_limit <= 0.0 or n <= 1:
        return best_a, float(2.0 * n * best_m)

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    end = t0 + float(t_limit)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    stall = 0
    fracs = (0.02, 0.05, 0.10)

    while time.monotonic() < end:
        c = conv_coeffs(a)
        m = float(c.max())
        if not np.isfinite(m):
            a = best_a.copy()
            c = conv_coeffs(a)
            m = float(c.max())

        if m < best_m - 1e-15:
            best_m = m
            best_a = a.copy()

        w = softmax_weights(c, 0.08)
        grad = gradient_weights(w, a)
        k = min(4, n)

        src_score = a * grad
        if k == n:
            srcs = np.arange(n)
        else:
            srcs = np.argpartition(src_score, k - 1)[-(k):]

        idx2 = 2 * np.arange(n)
        L = 2 * n - 1
        valid = idx2 < L
        selfc = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        selfc[valid] = c[idx2[valid]]
        pot = grad + 0.1 * a + 0.5 * selfc

        if k == n:
            dests = np.arange(n)
        else:
            dests = np.argpartition(pot, k - 1)[:k]

        improved = False
        for si in srcs:
            s = int(si)
            if a[s] <= 1e-10:
                continue
            for di in dests:
                d = int(di)
                if d == s:
                    continue
                for fr in fracs:
                    amt = a[s] * fr
                    if amt <= 0.0:
                        continue
                    t = a.copy()
                    t[s] -= amt
                    t[d] += amt
                    np.maximum(t, 0.0, out=t)
                    ts = float(t.sum())
                    if ts <= 1e-300:
                        continue
                    t = t / ts
                    mt = float(conv_coeffs(t).max())
                    if np.isfinite(mt) and mt < m - 1e-14:
                        a = t
                        m = mt
                        if m < best_m:
                            best_m = m
                            best_a = a.copy()
                        improved = True
                        break
                if improved:
                    break
            if improved:
                break

        if improved:
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall % 5 == 0:
                i = int(rng.integers(0, n))
                j = int(rng.integers(0, n))
                if i != j and a[i] > 1e-10:
                    amt = 0.02 * a[i]
                    t = a.copy()
                    t[i] -= amt
                    t[j] += amt
                    np.maximum(t, 0.0, out=t)
                    ts = float(t.sum())
                    if ts > 1e-300:
                        t = t / ts
                        mt = float(conv_coeffs(t).max())
                        if np.isfinite(mt) and mt < m:
                            a = t
                            m = mt
                            if m < best_m:
                                best_m = m
                                best_a = a.copy()
            if stall > 350:
                break

        if verbose and (stall % 200 == 1):
            print("  coord n=%d stall=%d score=%.7f" % (n, stall, 2.0 * best_m))

    return best_a, float(2.0 * n * best_m)


def random_smooth(n, seed):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    f = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for k in range(1, 9):
        amp = 0.35 / (float(k) ** 0.6)
        ph = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
        f = f + amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * float(k) * x + ph)
        amp2 = 0.25 / (float(k) ** 0.6)
        ph2 = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
        f = f + amp2 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * float(k) * x + ph2)
    lin = rng.uniform(-1.0, 1.0)
    f = f + lin * (x - 0.5)
    f = np.maximum(f, 1e-6)
    return normalize_seq(f)


def make_base_candidates(n):
    outs = []
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)

    outs.append(normalize_seq(np.ones(n)))
    outs.append(normalize_seq(x + 1e-12))
    outs.append(normalize_seq(1.0 - x + 1e-12))

    for p in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0):
        outs.append(normalize_seq(np.power(x, p) + 1e-12))
        outs.append(normalize_seq(np.power(1.0 - x, p) + 1e-12))

    for p in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
        vals = np.power(x, p) * np.power(1.0 - x, p) + 1e-12
        outs.append(normalize_seq(vals))

    for s in (-6.0, -3.0, -1.0, 1.0, 3.0, 6.0):
        outs.append(normalize_seq(np.exp(s * x)))

    for c in (0.15, 0.30, 0.50, 0.70, 0.85):
        for sg in (0.08, 0.18, 0.35):
            vals = np.exp(-0.5 * ((x - c) / sg) ** 2)
            outs.append(normalize_seq(vals))

    for c1, c2 in ((0.20, 0.80), (0.30, 0.70), (0.10, 0.90)):
        for sg in (0.10, 0.22):
            vals = (np.exp(-0.5 * ((x - c1) / sg) ** 2) +
                    np.exp(-0.5 * ((x - c2) / sg) ** 2))
            outs.append(normalize_seq(vals))

    for fq in (1, 2, 3, 5):
        vals = np.maximum(0.2 + np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * float(fq) * x), 1e-8)
        outs.append(normalize_seq(vals))
        vals = np.maximum(0.5 + 0.5 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * float(fq) * x), 1e-8)
        outs.append(normalize_seq(vals))

    for p in (-2.0, -1.0, -0.5, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
        for q in (-1.0, 0.0, 1.0):
            vals = 1.0 + p * (x - 0.5) + q * (x * (1.0 - x) - 1.0 / 6.0)
            vals = np.maximum(vals, 1e-8)
            outs.append(normalize_seq(vals))

    return outs


def make_small_candidates(n, parent):
    outs = []
    if parent is not None:
        outs.append(resample_seq(parent, n))
    outs.extend(make_base_candidates(n))
    for r in range(16):
        outs.append(random_smooth(n, 3000 + r))
    return outs


def make_main_candidates(n, parent, small_best):
    outs = []
    if parent is not None:
        outs.append(resample_seq(parent, n))
    if small_best is not None:
        outs.append(resample_seq(small_best, n))
    outs.extend(make_base_candidates(n))
    for r in range(6):
        outs.append(random_smooth(n, 5000 + r))
    return outs


def select_best_candidates(cands, limit):
    scored = []
    for idx, a in enumerate(cands):
        if a is None:
            continue
        a = normalize_seq(a)
        sc = score_seq(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored.append((float(sc), idx, a))
    if not scored:
        return []
    scored.sort()
    if len(scored) > limit:
        scored = scored[:limit]
    return [t[2] for t in scored]


def eval_safe(a):
    try:
        lst = [float(x) for x in a]
        v = evaluate_sequence(lst)
        if v is not None and np.isfinite(v):
            return float(v)
    except Exception:
        pass
    return float('inf')


def propose_candidate(budget_s=1800.0):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget = min(float(budget_s) - 90.0, 1710.0)
    except Exception:
        budget = 1710.0
    budget = max(budget, 30.0)

    print("budget=%.1f" % budget)
    parent = load_parent()

    actual_best = float('inf')
    actual_best_seq = None
    if parent is not None and budget > 500.0:
        pa = eval_safe(parent)
        if np.isfinite(pa):
            actual_best = pa
            actual_best_seq = parent.copy()
        print("parent actual=%.7f" % actual_best)

    # Small-scale model search.
    small_n = 1024 if budget > 800.0 else 512
    small_t = min(150.0, budget * 0.12)
    t_phase = time.monotonic()
    print("small search n=%d budget=%.1f" % (small_n, small_t))

    small_cands = make_small_candidates(small_n, parent)
    small_sel = select_best_candidates(small_cands, limit=6)

    best_small = None
    best_small_score = float('inf')
    if small_sel:
        n_run = min(len(small_sel), max(2, int(small_t // 15) + 1))
        per = small_t / max(1, n_run)
        for idx, a in enumerate(small_sel[:n_run]):
            if time.monotonic() - t_phase > small_t:
                break
            tt = min(per, 40.0)
            if tt < 2.0:
                tt = 2.0
            try:
                b, sc = run_peak_shaver(a, tt, seed=100 + idx,
                                        frac_start=0.5, frac_end=0.08,
                                        eta0=1.2, verbose=False)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_small_score:
                    best_small_score = sc
                    best_small = b
                    print("  small best=%.7f" % best_small_score)
            except Exception:
                pass

    # Main-length optimisation.
    main_n = 16384 if budget > 1000.0 else (12288 if budget > 500.0 else 8192)
    print("main n=%d" % main_n)
    main_cands = make_main_candidates(main_n, parent, best_small)
    main_sel = select_best_candidates(main_cands, limit=3)
    if not main_sel:
        main_sel = [np.ones(main_n, dtype=np.float64) / main_n]

    best_seq = normalize_seq(main_sel[0]).copy()
    best_score = score_seq(best_seq)
    print("initial main score=%.7f" % best_score)
    if best_score < actual_best:
        actual_best = best_score
        actual_best_seq = best_seq.copy()

    # Primary polished run on the best main candidate.
    main_shot = min(budget * 0.45, 720.0)
    t_phase = time.monotonic()
    if main_shot > 20.0:
        try:
            b, sc = run_peak_shaver(best_seq, main_shot, seed=7,
                                    frac_start=0.5, frac_end=0.05,
                                    eta0=1.0, verbose=True)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_seq = b
                print("primary improved=%.7f" % best_score)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Secondary candidate run.
    rem = budget - (time.monotonic() - t0)
    if len(main_sel) > 1 and rem > 100.0:
        tt = min(rem * 0.18, 220.0)
        try:
            b, sc = run_peak_shaver(main_sel[1], tt, seed=17,
                                    frac_start=0.45, frac_end=0.06,
                                    eta0=0.9, verbose=False)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_seq = b
                print("secondary improved=%.7f" % best_score)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Tertiary candidate run.
    rem = budget - (time.monotonic() - t0)
    if len(main_sel) > 2 and rem > 80.0:
        tt = min(rem * 0.12, 120.0)
        try:
            b, sc = run_peak_shaver(main_sel[2], tt, seed=27,
                                    frac_start=0.45, frac_end=0.06,
                                    eta0=0.9, verbose=False)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_seq = b
                print("tertiary improved=%.7f" % best_score)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Re-polish the current best with a slightly sharper continuation.
    rem = budget - (time.monotonic() - t0)
    if rem > 200.0:
        tt = min(rem * 0.25, 320.0)
        try:
            b, sc = run_peak_shaver(best_seq, tt, seed=37,
                                    frac_start=0.35, frac_end=0.03,
                                    eta0=0.7, verbose=True)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_seq = b
                print("repolish improved=%.7f" % best_score)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Frank-Wolfe-like direction method.
    rem = budget - (time.monotonic() - t0)
    if rem > 250.0:
        tt = min(rem * 0.30, 300.0)
        try:
            b, sc = fw_like(best_seq, tt, seed=47, verbose=False)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_seq = b
                print("fw improved=%.7f" % best_score)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Coordinate polish.
    rem = budget - (time.monotonic() - t0)
    if rem > 120.0:
        tt = min(rem * 0.35, 300.0)
        try:
            b, sc = coord_polish(best_seq, tt, seed=123, verbose=False)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_seq = b
                print("coord improved=%.7f" % best_score)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Try a slightly different final length for finite-size effects.
    rem = budget - (time.monotonic() - t0)
    if rem > 180.0:
        n_extra = 12288 if main_n > 12288 else 8192
        tt = min(rem * 0.40, 160.0)
        try:
            a_ex = resample_seq(best_seq, n_extra)
            b, sc = run_peak_shaver(a_ex, tt, seed=57,
                                    frac_start=0.45, frac_end=0.05,
                                    eta0=0.9, verbose=False)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_seq = b
                print("extra length improved=%.7f n=%d" % (best_score, n_extra))
        except Exception:
            pass

    best_seq = normalize_seq(best_seq)

    # Final actual-evaluator validation. If the evaluator is unusually slow or
    # the sequence is too long, gently resample once and validate again.
    fs = eval_safe(best_seq)
    final_seq = best_seq
    final_actual = fs
    if not np.isfinite(final_actual):
        if len(best_seq) > 12288:
            alt = resample_seq(best_seq, 12288)
            fs2 = eval_safe(alt)
            if np.isfinite(fs2):
                final_seq = alt
                final_actual = fs2
        if not np.isfinite(final_actual):
            final_seq = np.ones(4096, dtype=np.float64) / 4096.0
            final_actual = eval_safe(final_seq)

    # Safety fallback to the previous state of the art if evaluation says so.
    if actual_best_seq is not None and np.isfinite(actual_best) and actual_best < final_actual:
        final_seq = actual_best_seq
        final_actual = actual_best

    final_seq = normalize_seq(final_seq)
    if len(final_seq) > 18000:
        final_seq = resample_seq(final_seq, 16384)

    ff = eval_safe(final_seq)
    if not np.isfinite(ff):
        final_seq = np.ones(4096, dtype=np.float64) / 4096.0
        ff = eval_safe(final_seq)
    if not np.isfinite(ff):
        final_seq = np.ones(2048, dtype=np.float64) / 2048.0

    print("FINAL local=%.7f actual=%.7f n=%d t=%.1fs" %
          (best_score, final_actual, len(final_seq), time.monotonic() - t0))

    return [float(x) for x in final_seq]
```
